function __doPostBack(eventTarget, eventArgument) {
    if (!theForm.onsubmit || (theForm.onsubmit() != false)) {
        theForm.__EVENTTARGET.value = eventTarget;
        theForm.__EVENTARGUMENT.value = eventArgument;
        theForm.submit();
    }
}

</script>

<input autocomplete="off" class="ui-autocomplete-input" id="txtSearchTerm" placeholder="zde napište co hledáte..." type="text"/>

<script>
  var suggestLocalization = {
    'hotel':'hotel',
    'destination':'CKFicher.SuggestLocalization.Destination',
    'other':'ostatní'
  }
</script>
<script>
var cx = '009641810464511143812:lek6nmtenpw'; // search engine id 
var apiKey = 'AIzaSyAmSRSwvnl-jV5y22M6qhy_CMT1N_e8Zi8'; // api key

$(function () {
  $('#btnSearch').click(function () {
    var searchStr = $("#txtSearchTerm").val();
    $(location).attr('href', '/hledani-vysledky?q=' + searchStr);
  });
  $("#txtSearchTerm").on("keypress", function(e) {
    if(window.event && window.event.keyCode == 13) {
      var searchStr = $("#txtSearchTerm").val();
      $(location).attr('href', '/hledani-vysledky?q=' + searchStr);
    }
  })

 //  $("form").submit(function (e) {
 //    e.preventDefault();
 //  })

  var cache = {};
  $("#txtSearchTerm").autocomplete({
    minLength: 100,
    delay: 500,
    source: function (request, response) {
      var term = request.term;

      $.getJSON("https://www.googleapis.com/customsearch/v1/siterestrict?key=" +
        apiKey + "&num=10&cx=" + cx + "&q=" + term + "&callback=?", request,
        function (data, status, xhr) {
          var parsedData = [];
          $.each(data.spelling, function(key, value){
              if (key === "correctedQuery")
                parsedData.push(value);
          });
          response(parsedData);
          $(".ui-menu-item").on("click", function (e) {
            $(location).attr('href', '/hledani-vysledky?q=' + $(".ui-menu-item").text());
          });
        });
    }
  });
});
</script>

<h2 class="fshr-title fshr-title--pageSection fshr-title--topCuddle">Akční týden: Rhodos a Emiráty</h2>
<div class="js-countDown-active">
<p class="fshr-paragraph fshr-countDown-text">Na tento týden jsme pro vás připravili akční nabídku <br/><strong>nejoblíbenějších hotelů na Rhodosu a v Arabských emirátech za speciální ceny.</strong><br/>

Akce je časově omezena a platí <strong>pouze od 22. 4. do 28. 4. 2019.</strong>
</p>
<p class="fshr-paragraph fshr-countDown-title">Do konce akce zbývá</p>
<div class="fshr-countDown js-hpCountDown" data-countdowntill="2019-04-28T23:59:59">
<span class="fshr-countDown-item js-hpCountDown-item" data-countdown-suffix="Den,Dny,Dní" data-countdown-type="day">
<span class="fshr-countDown-value js-hpCountDown-value"></span>
<span class="fshr-countDown-unit js-hpCountDown-suffix">Dny</span>
</span>

<span class="fshr-countDown-value js-hpCountDown-value"></span>
<span class="fshr-countDown-unit js-hpCountDown-suffix">Hodina</span>
</span>
<span class="fshr-countDown-item js-hpCountDown-item" data-countdown-suffix="Minuta,Minuty,Minut" data-countdown-type="minute">
<span class="fshr-countDown-value js-hpCountDown-value"></span>
<span class="fshr-countDown-unit js-hpCountDown-suffix">Minuty</span>
</span>
<span class="fshr-countDown-item js-hpCountDown-item" data-countdown-suffix="Sekunda,Sekundy,Sekund" data-countdown-type="second">

<script>
    var milisecPerSecond = 1000;
    var milisecPerMinute = 60 * milisecPerSecond;
    var milisecPerHour = 60 * milisecPerMinute;
    var milisecPerDay = 24 * milisecPerHour;

    function hpCountDown (tillDate, $countDownItems) {
        var currentDate = new Date().getTime();
        var remainingTime = tillDate - currentDate;

        if (remainingTime > milisecPerSecond) {
            var days = Math.floor(remainingTime / milisecPerDay);
            remainingTime = remainingTime - (days * milisecPerDay);
            var hours = Math.floor(remainingTime / milisecPerHour);
            remainingTime = remainingTime - (hours * milisecPerHour);
            var minutes = Math.floor(remainingTime / milisecPerMinute);
            remainingTime = remainingTime - (minutes * milisecPerMinute);
            var seconds = Math.floor(remainingTime / milisecPerSecond);

            $countDownItems.each(function () {
                var $item = $(this);
                var type = $item.attr("data-countdown-type");
                var suffixValues = $item.attr("data-countdown-suffix");

                var $suffix = $item.find(".js-hpCountDown-suffix");
                var $value = $item.find(".js-hpCountDown-value");
                var value = $value.html();
                switch (type) {
                    case "day":
                        value = days;
                        break;
                    case "hour":
                        value = hours;
                        $value.html(hours);
                        break;
                    case "minute":
                        value = minutes;
                        break;
                    case "second":
                        value = seconds;
                        break;
                }
                $value.html(value);
                $suffix.html(window.fshr.getProperSuffix(value, suffixValues));
            });
            setTimeout(function () {
                hpCountDown (tillDate, $countDownItems);
            }, 1000);
        } else {
            var $parent = $countDownItems.closest(".js-countDownAction");
            $parent.find(".js-countDown-active").hide();
            $parent.find(".js-countDown-inactive").show();
        }

    }
    $(document).on("ready", function () {

        var $countDown = $(".js-hpCountDown");
        var $countDownParent = $countDown.closest(".js-countDownAction");

        var countDownTimestamp = $countDown.attr("data-countdowntill");
        if (!countDownTimestamp) {
            $countDownParent.hide();
        } else {
            var tillDate = new Date(countDownTimestamp).getTime();
            var currentDate = new Date().getTime();
            var $countDownItems = $countDown.find(".js-hpCountDown-item");
            console.log(tillDate, currentDate);
            if (tillDate > currentDate) {
                $countDownParent.show();
                hpCountDown(tillDate, $countDownItems);
            }
        }

    });
</script><div class="f_teaser f_teaser--cascade">
